local function c3(
v,
f)if typeof(
v)=="Color3"then
 return v end

v)=="ColorSequence"then

v)=="table"then

v.Color,
f)end

 return c3(
v["50"],
f)end

 return c3(
v["100"],
f)end
 if v["0"]then
 return c3(
v["0"],
f)end
 end
 return f or Color3.new(
1,
1,
1)end

local function makeConfig(
n)ensureDir(
)local c={
Path=CD.."/"..n..".json",
_data={
}
,
CustomData={
}
,
Elements=CM.Elements}
function c:Get(
k)return self._data[k]end
 function c:Set(
k,
v)self._data[k]=v end
 function c:SetAsCurrent(
)Lib._currentConfig=c end
 function c:Register(
)end
 function c:Save(
)pcall(
function(
)local out={
__version=1,
__elements={
}
,
__custom=self.CustomData}
for flag,
el in pairs(
CM.Elements)do
 local parser=CM.Parser[el.__type or el.Type]if parser then
 local ok,
data=pcall(
parser.Save,
el)if ok and data then
 out.__elements[flag]=data end
 end
 end
 for k,
v in pairs(
self._data)do
 if k~="__version"and k~="__elements"and k~="__custom"then
 out[k]=v end
 end
 writefile(
self.Path,
HS:JSONEncode(
out))end
)end
 function c:Delete(
)pcall(
function(
)if isfile and isfile(
self.Path)then
 delfile(
self.Path)end
 end
)end
 function c:Load(
)pcall(
function(
)if not(
isfile and isfile(
self.Path))then
 return end
 local data=HS:JSONDecode(
readfile(
self.Path))if type(
data)~="table"then
 return end
 if data.__version then
 self.CustomData=data.__custom or{
}
for k,
v in pairs(
data)do
 if k~="__version"and k~="__elements"and k~="__custom"then
 self._data[k]=v end
 end
 end
 end
)end
 return c end

function CM:CreateConfig(
n)if self._configs[n]then
 return self._configs[n]end
 local c=makeConfig(
n)c:Load(
)self._configs[n]=c return c end

function CM:GetConfig(
n)return self._configs[n]end

function CM:AllConfigs(
)ensureDir(
)local names={
}
pcall(
function(
)if not listfiles then
 return end
 for _,
path in ipairs(
listfiles(
CD))do
 local n=path:match(
"([^/\\]+)%.json$")if n then
 names[#names+1]=n if not self._configs[n]then
 local c=makeConfig(
n)c:Load(
)self._configs[n]=c end
 end
 end
 end
)for k in pairs(
self._configs)do
 if not table.find(
names,
k)then
 names[#names+1]=k end
 end
 table.sort(
names)return names end

function tb:UpdateHeight(
)if vt.Frame then
 local z=vt.Frame:FindFirstChild(
"ZbColumns")if z then
 vt.Frame.CanvasSize=UDim2.new(
0,
0,
0,
z.AbsoluteSize.Y+10)elseif
 vt.Frame:FindFirstChildOfClass(
"UIGridLayout")then
 vt.Frame.AutomaticCanvasSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.Y vt.Frame.CanvasSize=UDim2.new(
0,
0,
0,
0)else
 local h=0 for i,
v in next,
self.Sections do
 h=h+v:GetHeight(
)+(
i<#self.Sections and 5 or 0)end
 vt.Frame.CanvasSize=UDim2.new(
0,
0,
0,
h)end
 end
 end

function tb:GetHeight(
)local z=vt.Frame and vt.Frame:FindFirstChild(
"ZbColumns")if z then
 return z.AbsoluteSize.Y+10 end
 local h=0 for i,
v in next,
self.Sections do
 h=h+v:GetHeight(
)+(
i<#self.Sections and 5 or 0)end
 return h end

return tb end

task.spawn(
function(
)
    while not g.unloaded do

        task.wait(
) -- Runs every frame to completely eliminate the flashing/closing effect

        local sb = win.Frame:FindFirstChild(
"Sidebar")
        local bg = win.Frame:FindFirstChild(
"Background")
        if sb then

            if not sb.Visible then
 sb.Visible = true end

            if sb.Size.X.Offset < 100 then

                sb.Size = UDim2.new(
0,
 140,
 1,
 0)
                if bg then

                    -- Moved the Y position down  35 pixels so it doesn't cover the title bar

                    bg.Position = UDim2.new(
0,
 140,
 0,
 35)
                    bg.Size = UDim2.new(
1,
 -140,
 1,
 -35)
                end

            end

            for _,
v in ipairs(
win.Frame:GetDescendants(
)) do

                if (
v:IsA(
"TextButton") and v.Text == "+") or (
v:IsA(
"ImageButton") and v.Name:lower(
):find(
"mobile")) then

                    v.Visible = false
                    v.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(
0,
0) -- Destroys the button size so it can never be clicked

                end

            end

        end

    end

end
)
